## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单仅 westd-34887 的 **cuda:0 + cuda:1**，绝不能用其他卡
- 所有训练命令必须带 `WANDB_MODE=offline`（服务器无 wandb API key，不加直接崩）
- KL PPL 论文引用统一用 **4-seed mean 12.00±0.07**，禁用单 seed 最佳值 11.91
- BCE framing 禁止用 "anti-learning"，必须用 **"objective mismatch"**
- SPON framing 禁止 "KL subsumes SPON"，用 **"marginal complement"**
- D032 TEAL 30% PPL = **8.98** 是正确值，13.63 是 50% target 错误修正已撤回
- submit_training_job 必须自带环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: westd-34887，工作目录 `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`
- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base，不是 venv）
- **模型**: Llama-3.1-8B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b/`，Mistral-7B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`
- **Checkpoints**: KL30%=`checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`，KL40%=`checkpoints/kl_sparsity40_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`，KL50%=`checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`
- **活跃进程**:
  - bench_kl50_uniform: PID 229957, cuda:0, 50% KL uniform benchmark eval
  - bench_kl40_global: PID 230101, cuda:1, 40% KL global benchmark eval
- **提交时间**: 12:20 UTC，预计 75-120min 完成（约 13:35-14:20 UTC）
- **累计 GPU·h**: ~92h / 预算 ~196h

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚完成 bench_teal30 + bench_teal50 的分析和 Director 汇报。已向 Director 发送完整 Table 1 对比表（8/10 格已填），请求下一批实验优先级。

- **RUNNING (2)**: bench_kl50_uniform (cuda:0), bench_kl40_global (cuda:1)
- **PREPARING (7)**: kl_lagrangian_v2, kl_norm_v3_quicktest, mvp_kl_norm_s456, spon_kl_s42, diag_30pct_downstream, diag_gsm8k_gencomp, diag_teal_downstream
- **等待**: Director 回复下一批实验优先级；两个 bench 的 cron 回调

### 4. 📋 待办
- bench_kl50_uniform 完成 → 记录 metrics → Table 1 uniform 列完整（预期：50% uniform 仍 catastrophic）
- bench_kl40_global 完成 → 记录 metrics → Table 1 global 列完整 → **3×2 matrix 全部齐备**
- 两个都完成 → 两块 GPU 空闲 → 等 Director 指示下一批（已发请求）
- Director 回复 → 从 PREPARING 中选实验提交（建议 P0: diag_30pct_downstream / diag_teal_downstream）
- 全 matrix 齐备 + Director 同意 → spawn Worker 开始写 Results section

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**Table 1 已收集数据**:
| 条件 | PPL | ARC | WG | MMLU | GSM8K |
|------|-----|-----|-----|------|-------|
| Dense | 6.12 | 58.3 | 78.1 | 65.3 | 48.8 |
| TEAL 30% | 10.97 | 42.3 | 66.1 | 38.6 | 7.9 |
| TEAL 50% | 23.31 | 28.4 | 53.0 | 28.3 | 2.0 |
| KL 30% global | 8.98 | 39.7 | 70.2 | 25.3 | 8.8 |
| KL 30% uniform | 8.82 | 40.8 | 68.6 | 40.6 | 7.4 |
| KL 40% uniform | 8.36 | 41.8 | 67.2 | 30.7 | 3.9 |
| KL 50% global | 11.79 | 32.9 | 63.5 | 27.9 | 1.3 |
| BCE 50% | 22.51 | 24.0 | 50.6 | 30.0 | 4.9 |

**核心结论链**: KL>>TEAL on PPL → uniform 修复 MMLU at 30% → 40% phase transition → 50% 不可救

**易踩坑**:
1. global top-k 和 uniform allocation 的结果不能混在一起比——它们是不同推理协议
2. TEAL vanilla 30% MMLU=38.6% 比预想好——但 PPL 差距仍然显著（10.97 vs 8.82）
3. 50% 不要再试任何 allocation 方法——已验证三种方法全部崩溃
4. DDL 2026-05-25（剩 9 天），Table 1 完成后立即转论文写作
5. Mistral 数据疑似污染（D032），暂不引用

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory — distill_sparse_swiglu\\n\\n## 关键决策原因\\n\\n### Uniform Allocation 为何是论文推荐配置\\n- Global top-k 将 sparsity 集中在后层（Layer 26-31: 41-70%），导致 MMLU 崩溃至 25.3%\\n- Uniform per-layer allocation 强制每层同 sparsity，30% 时 MMLU 恢复至 40.6%\\n- 机制解释：后层承载知识检索，过度剪枝直接破坏 factual recall\\n\\n### Phase Transition 而非 Quadratic Scaling (D034)\\n- 原 Claim 3 用 \\\"quadratic scaling\\\" 描述 sparsity 增加时 downstream 退化\\n- 数据显示 30→40% 是 cliff（MMLU 40.6%→30.7%），不是连续退化\\n- Reframe 为 \\\"phase transition\\\"：存在临界点，跨过后不可修复\\n\\n### BCE Framing: \\\"Objective Mismatch\\\" (非 Anti-Learning)\\n- BCE 训练 per-neuron binary classification（每个神经元独立判断是否激活）\\n- 推理时用 global top-k 选择（全局排序取 top）\\n- 训练目标（独立 binary）和推理协议（全局 top-k）结构性不兼容\\n- \\\"Anti-learning\\\" 暗示模型学错了，实际是目标函数和推理协议的 mismatch\\n\\n### SPON Framing: \\\"Marginal Complement\\\" (非 KL Subsumes SPON)\\n- SPON 提供正交信号（结构先验），KL 无法 subsume 因为二者优化不同目标\\n- 正确描述：SPON 在 KL 基础上提供 marginal complement（边际补充）\\n\\n## 约束\\n\\n### 服务器环境\\n- westd-34887, conda base 环境, `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`\\n- 工作目录: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`\\n- 模型: Llama-3.1-8B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b/`\\n- Mistral-7B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`\\n- WANDB_MODE=offline（服务器无 wandb API key）\\n- GPU 白名单: cuda:0 + cuda:1 only\\n\\n### 数据红线\\n- KL PPL 引用: 4-seed mean 12.00±0.07（禁用单 seed 最佳 11.91）\\n- D032 TEAL 30% PPL = 8.98（13.63 是 50% target 的结果，D032 错误修正已撤回）\\n- Mistral registry 数据疑似污染（D032），暂不引用\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n### Benchmark 一致性 (D029)\\n- 论文所有对比必须用相同 few-shot 设置（ARC-c=25, WG=5, MMLU=5, GSM8K=8）\\n- 不同 shot 数的结果不可直接对比\\n- bench_dense 是统一 baseline，所有新 bench 对齐它的设置\\n\\n### 50% Uniform 无法拯救\\n- 50% uniform MMLU=27.77% ≈ random\\n- 不要再尝试 allocation 方法拯救 50%，已验证不可能\\n\\n### 不要凭推理改数字 (D032 教训)\\n- 修正数据前必须复现验证\\n- D032 曾错误推理 30% TEAL PPL 为 13.63，实际 8.98 是正确值\\n\\n## Checkpoints 路径\\n- KL 30%: `checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\\n- KL 40%: `checkpoints/kl_sparsity40_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\\n- KL 50%: `checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\\n- BCE v3: `checkpoints/bce_isocompute_v3_s42/`\\n\\n## 论文 Narrative\\n- 骨架: PPL-Downstream Disconnect（文献空白最大）\\n- 血肉: Sparsity Threshold（30% 可修复，50% 不可修复）\\n- DDL: EMNLP 2026 ARR May, 2026-05-25（剩 9 天）\\n- A+C 混合 framing，Director 已批准\\n\\n## Table 1 数据完成度 (2026-05-16 12:30 UTC)\\n- Dense ✅ | TEAL 30% ✅ | TEAL 50% ✅\\n- KL 30% global ✅ | KL 30% uniform ✅\\n- KL 40% global ⏳ | KL 40% uniform ✅  \\n- KL 50% global ✅ | KL 50% uniform ⏳\\n- BCE 50% ✅\\n\", \"total_chars\": 2316, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

  "content": "# Memory — distill_sparse_swiglu\n\n## 关键决策原因\n\n### Uniform Allocation 为何是论文推荐配置\n- Global top-k 将 sparsity 集中在后层（Layer 26-31: 41-70%），导致 MMLU 崩溃至 25.3%\n- Uniform per-layer allocation 强制每层同 sparsity，30% 时 MMLU 恢复至 40.6%\n- 机制解释：后层承载知识检索，过度剪枝直接破坏 factual recall\n\n### Phase Transition 而非 Quadratic Scaling (D034)\n- 原 Claim 3 用 \"quadratic scaling\" 描述 sparsity 增加时 downstream 退化\n- 数据显示 30→40% 是 cliff（MMLU 40.6%→30.7%），不是连续退化\n- Reframe 为 \"phase transition\"：存在临界点，跨过后不可修复\n\n### BCE Framing: \"Objective Mismatch\" (非 Anti-Learning)\n- BCE 训练 per-neuron binary classification（每个神经元独立判断是否激活）\n- 推理时用 global top-k 选择（全局排序取 top）\n- 训练目标（独立 binary）和推理协议（全局 top-k）结构性不兼容\n- \"Anti-learning\" 暗示模型学错了，实际是目标函数和推理协议的 mismatch\n\n### SPON Framing: \"Marginal Complement\" (非 KL Subsumes SPON)\n- SPON 提供正交信号（结构先验），KL 无法 subsume 因为二者优化不同目标\n- 正确描述：SPON 在 KL 基础上提供 marginal complement（边际补充）\n\n### KL 40% Global MMLU Anomaly (D041)\n- PPL=10.12（比 uniform 8.36 差 21%）但 MMLU=38.1%（比 uniform 30.7% 好 +7.4pp）\n- 与 30% 时模式相反（30% global MMLU=25.3% < uniform 40.6%）\n- 可能解释：40% global 的 sparsity 分配恰好保护了知识层（需进一步验证）\n- 论文中标注但不过度解释\n\n## 约束\n\n### 服务器环境\n- westd-34887, conda base 环境, `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`\n- 工作目录: `/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`\n- 模型: Llama-3.1-8B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/llama-3.1-8b/`\n- Mistral-7B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`\n- WANDB_MODE=offline（服务器无 wandb API key）\n- GPU 白名单: cuda:0 + cuda:1 only\n\n### 数据红线\n- KL PPL 引用: 4-seed mean 12.00±0.07（禁用单 seed 最佳 11.91）\n- D032 TEAL 30% PPL = 8.98（13.63 是 50% target 的结果，D032 错误修正已撤回）\n- Mistral registry 数据疑似污染（D032），暂不引用\n\n## 经验教训\n\n### Benchmark 一致性 (D029)\n- 论文所有对比必须用相同 few-shot 设置（ARC-c=25, WG=5, MMLU=5, GSM8K=8）\n- 不同 shot 数的结果不可直接对比\n- bench_dense 是统一 baseline，所有新 bench 对齐它的设置\n\n### 50% Uniform 无法拯救\n- 50% uniform MMLU=27.77% ≈ random\n- 不要再尝试 allocation 方法拯救 50%，已验证不可能\n\n### 不要凭推理改数字 (D032 教训)\n- 修正数据前必须复现验证\n- D032 曾错误推理 30% TEAL PPL 为 13.63，实际 8.98 是正确值\n\n## Checkpoints 路径\n- KL 30%: `checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42_v3/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\n- KL 40%: `checkpoints/kl_sparsity40_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\n- KL 50%: `checkpoints/mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt`\n- BCE v3: `checkpoints/bce_isocompute_v3_s42/`\n\n## 论文 Narrative\n- 骨架: PPL-Downstream Disconnect（文献空白最大）\n- 血肉: Sparsity Threshold（30% 可修复，50% 不可修复）\n- DDL: EMNLP 2026 ARR May, 2026-05-25（剩 9 天）\n- A+C 混合 framing，Director 已批准\n\n## Table 1 数据完成度 (2026-05-16 17:00 UTC)\n- Dense ✅ | TEAL 30% ✅ | TEAL 50% ✅\n- KL 30% global ✅ | KL 30% uniform ✅\n- KL 40% global ✅ | KL 40% uniform ✅\n- KL 50% global ✅ | KL 50% uniform ✅ (3-seed: std=0.07)\n- BCE 50% ✅\n- **全部完成 ✅**\n"
}
